(1) `CelestialMath.getCelestialDirection` memoizes the `Astronomy.Equator` solar/lunar solve — every sky sitch called it 6–10× per frame *per view* with an identical (body, instant, observer) triplet; cache keyed on body + epoch ms + observer position quantised to ~1 km. (2) `CNodeTrackAir.update` replaces an O(frames²)-per-playback wind prefix-sum re-run with an O(1) read of a `cumulativeWind[]` array now baked in `recalculate()`. (3) `CCustomManager` pre/postRenderUpdate caches its `CNode3DObject` list gated on `NodeMan.listVersion` instead of scanning the whole ~300-node graph once per visible 3D view per frame. Result: smoother playback in sky and custom sitches.

### Bug Fixes
- **Traverse analysis no longer fits constructed sightlines ("bad balloon fits")** (`3e61d42f`) (`src/AnalyzeTraverse.js`, `src/TrackManager.js`, `src/TraverseAnalysis.js`, `src/trackSourceUtils.js`). Root cause: a MISB LOS re-import force-set Camera Heading to "To Target", so the analysis consumed sightlines *constructed* from the smoothed Center track (circular), and with a frozen camera those bearings had no parallax — every free-range method collapsed toward the sensor (8–11 km truth error on a 5.3 km scene). Fixed in layers: import (`shouldPreserveAnglesHeading`) keeps the camera track's recorded sensor angles when the arriving auto-target is that same track's derived Center frame-center track; an analysis safety net substitutes the measured `Track_<name>_LOS` (recorded az/el) when the selected LOS is the circular plain-boresight (never for pixel-tracking sources, whose offset is real measurement), with provenance threaded through the fingerprint/manifest; `sensorMotionStats`/`isRangeUnobservable` uses the bbox-diagonal baseline (not GPS-jitter path length) and surfaces an unmissable "range is NOT determined by this evidence" banner; and `pickConstAirRegime` prefers the slow range-profile valley over the 380 kt-prior sweep only when speed ≤ ~100 kt with real contrast and a margin win (balloon constAir truth error 3,536 m → 129 m). Gallery/verdict/report banners and an apply-toast note the measured-LOS substitution.
- **UWYO soundings load again (dead cgi-bin endpoint)** (`bf4ba091`) (`sitrecServer/proxySounding.php`, `src/ParseSonde.js`, `src/TrackManager.js`). UWYO removed the legacy `cgi-bin/sounding` endpoint (404 as of July 2026), which broke `TEXT:LIST` fetches including on production; both CSV and LIST formats now route through the WSGI endpoint. `parseUWYOList` now parses the WSGI page's station metadata (H1/H3/I header block) and "at HH UTC" observation time — previously a WSGI page parsed to station (0,0) and time "now", putting the sonde track on the equator with a misdated profile. Sonde tracks are also treated as reference wind data: importing one no longer re-times the sitch to the balloon launch nor marks the sitch established (which would have blocked the next real track from setting time/location).
- **Fixed the Wind Source dropdown checkmark stuck on the wrong option** (`aceaedcf`) (`src/lil-gui-extras.js`). The Wind Source dropdown displayed "UWYO Soundings" and held the correct value, but its native `<select>` was stuck with the checkmark on the first option (GFS), so opening it showed GFS ticked and clicking GFS did nothing. lil-gui's `OptionController.updateDisplay` short-circuits when the value is unchanged; when `.options()` rebuilds the `<select>` (the wind dropdown rebuilds as soundings finish loading, adding "Track:…" entries) the recreated select defaults `selectedIndex` to 0, but the unchanged-value guard kept `updateDisplay`/`.listen()` from ever re-pointing it. Fixed by clearing `_lastDisplayedValue` before `updateDisplay` in the three places that mutate a dropdown's options or programmatically set its value — `setValueQuietly`, `addOptionToGUIMenu`, `removeOptionFromGUIMenu`.
- **Fixed black main-view sky in Chrome 150 development builds** (`f5c21f00`) (`src/index.js`) — *development builds only; production builds exclude the IWER emulator and were never affected.* IWER's `installRuntime()` auto-ran on every local dev page load, spoofing a Quest 3 user agent and installing an emulated WebXR runtime; as of Chrome 150 those hooks make every alpha-blended pass into a multisampled render target silently vanish, so the sky quad and night-sky stars disappeared from the MSAA target while opaque terrain survived, turning the dev-build main-view sky permanently black in Chrome (Edge's older Chromium unaffected). The emulator now installs only when `?iwer=1` is in the URL, keeping local WebXR testing available without hooking every dev session.
- **MCP-driven hidden tabs no longer stall** (`3d3ab5ec`) (`src/index.js`) — *MCP/SitrecBridge automation only; no effect on regular user sessions.* A backgrounded Sitrec tab driven via SitrecBridge stalled (Chrome fires no rAF in hidden tabs and clamps their timers, and the render-on-demand loop force-slept on `document.hidden`), so terrain never subdivided and MCP tests hung until the tab was focused. When the bridge is attached (`_mcpDebug`), the sleep gates now treat hidden as visible and a tiny Blob-worker heartbeat (worker timers are exempt from hidden-tab throttling) fires pending scheduled frames at ~30 fps; an idle background tab still sleeps via render-on-demand.
- **Fixed z-fighting / terraced terrain in orthographic views** (`108166d9`) (`src/Globe.js`, `src/ModelLoader.js`, `src/js/map33/material/TerrainDayNightMaterial.js`, `src/nodes/CNode3DLight.js`, `src/nodes/CNode3DObjectGeometry.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeDisplayWindField.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeGroundOverlay.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeSpriteGroup.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeSynthClouds.js`, `src/nodes/CPointLightCloud.js`). About a dozen custom fragment shaders (twelve depth-write sites across ten files) hand-roll Three.js-style logarithmic depth and write `gl_FragDepthEXT` from clip-space w (`vPosition.w` / `vDepth`, i.e. `gl_Position.w`). In an orthographic projection w is a constant 1.0 for every fragment, so the log formula collapses to a single depth value for the whole surface — catastrophic z-fighting that rendered terrain as flat-topped terraces and would corrupt models (gradient-shaded objects, PLY point clouds, Gaussian splats), sprites, synthetic clouds, ground overlays, the wind field, the day/night globe, and point lights the same way. Each site now falls back to the rasteriser's linear depth (`gl_FragCoord.z`) when `w == 1.0` — mirroring Three.js's own `logdepthbuf_fragment` behaviour for non-perspective matrices — with the ground-overlay site preserving its `depthBias`. The perspective (else) branch is byte-identical, so perspective rendering and regression baselines are unaffected. (Orthographic views are toggled from Camera → Camera Tweaks → **Main View Orthographic** / **Look View Orthographic**, added in 2.100.0.)
- **Fixed the orthographic box sinking underground on shallow/grazing views** (`c847f881`) (`src/nodes/CNodeCamera.js`). The ortho box half-height (`_updateOrthographicProjectionMatrix`, `halfH`) is sized to match the perspective framing at the screen-centre ground distance `d`. On a shallow, grazing view `d` far exceeds the camera's height above ground, so the box's lower edge dropped kilometres underground; the bottom of the screen then sampled terrain backfaces/skirts at grazing incidence, appearing as vertical picket-fence streaks with a gap where near ground should be. `halfH` is now clamped to `camAGL / (up·localUp)` (new per-frame caches `_orthoCamAGL` and `_orthoUpDotRadial`, applied only when `upDotRadial > 1e-3`), holding the box's lower edge at/above the ground beneath the camera; because up·localUp → 0 for a top-down view, the clamp only bites on shallow tilts and leaves top-down / high-altitude ortho unchanged. The commit also widens the ortho raycaster's layer mask to the camera's (`this._orthoRaycaster.layers.mask = cam.layers.mask`) before every ground query — a fresh `Raycaster` only tests layer 0, but terrain lives on the camera's render layers, so the camera-to-ground sizing distance (and the new downward AGL query) were silently falling back to the ellipsoid horizon instead of the real terrain mesh.

## Version 2.101.0 (2026-07-11)

### New Features
- **Traverse-analysis gallery reworked around grouped ranking — no global winner** (`bcb179df`, `86ba9f9c`) (`src/AnalyzeTraverse.js`, `src/TraverseRanking.js`, `src/TraverseAnalysisCache.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeTerrain.js`). The **Analyze Traverse Methods...** button (Traverse menu) now runs the full hypothesis battery — constant-air-speed sweep with family bands and geometric bracket expansion (`sweepConstAirSpeed`), minimum-acceleration range profile with separate range/speed edge reporting, physics fits, stationary/ground/astronomical checks — into a gallery ranked by `groupAndRankHypotheses` (`src/TraverseRanking.js`) which orders hypotheses only *within* four comparison groups ("Geometric and LOS-constrained trajectory families", "Object-conditioned forward models", "Known-object catalogue checks", "Estimator diagnostics"), replacing 2.100.0's single plausibility-descending ranking across unlike explanations. Every tile always shows its raw LOS residual; completeness-aware ordering plus badges surface caveats ("Search incomplete", "Model limit hit", "Unconstrained at bound", "Internal clamp reached", "Optimizer incomplete"). When the sightlines are reconstructed rather than measured, a provenance banner ("⚠ Constructed LOS — validation only") brands the run in the overlay and the report. The printable report is built lazily and carries a run-audit manifest; whole runs are cached (`TraverseAnalysisCache.js`) keyed by every scientific input — including a new `CNodeTerrain.elevationRevision` content counter, the star-catalog load state, and `simSpeed`/fps — but deliberately NOT render-camera LOD. Review hardening folded in pre-release (never shipped broken): the contender force-refresh handles the `CNodeTrack` lazy-bake pattern, so a `_dirty`-only check can no longer serve a stale Straight-Line tile to "Use exact".
- **"Use exact result" installs an Analysis Snapshot** (`bcb179df`, `8bc109f8`) (`src/AnalyzeTraverse.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeLOSFitAnalysisResult.js`, `src/MakeTraverseNodesMenu.js`). The gallery's **Use exact result** button installs the analyzed track itself — not a re-run of a live method — as a new traverse option, shown in the **LOS Traverse Method** dropdown as **Analysis Snapshot (created by Analyze)**. `CNodeLOSFitAnalysisResult` serializes and restores the exact analyzed ENU sample array, so the reviewed trajectory survives save/load byte-for-byte and never re-solves.
- **Live Global Fits follow the In/Out (A-B) window** (`8bc109f8`, `86ba9f9c`, `2a62da42`, `bcb179df`) (`src/nodes/CNodeLOSFit*.js`, `src/TraverseAnalysisData.js`, `src/MakeTraverseNodesMenu.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeCurveEdit2.js`, `src/updateFrame.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeDateTime.js`). Every live Global Fit node (`CNodeLOSFitCA/CV/Kalman/MinSpeed/MonteCarlo/MonteCarlo2/Physics/Plausible` and the new stationary/ground nodes) fits only the In/Out window via the shared `abFrameRange` authority in `TraverseAnalysisData.js` — the same range the analysis gallery fits — and holds endpoint positions outside it. Every path that mutates `Sit.aFrame`/`bFrame` dispatches `abFrameChanged` so the fits refresh: the I/O keys, frame-slider marker drags, graph-view marker drags (`CNodeCurveEdit2.onMouseUp`), the "G" go-to-frame prompt (`updateFrame.js`), and the Sim Speed slider (`CNodeDateTime`, which also rebakes the graph because `Sit.simSpeed` is a hidden global input with no graph edge). The `MakeTraverseNodesMenu` listener invalidates only the `LOSFit*` inputs (skipping the Analysis Snapshot) rather than rebaking the whole graph. Short-window hardening (new behavior, never shipped broken): `trackMetrics` clamps its smoothing window so 10–18-frame A-B selections report real speed/g metrics, and empty ranges read NaN (= "Invalid metrics" in the ranking) instead of 0.00 g that passed the broad screen. The sequential Constant Altitude traverse deliberately anchors on the clip's FIRST ray, not the In point (`CNodeLOSTraverseConstantAltitude.js` — shipped GoFast sets `aFrame: 375` purely to skip the pre-lock segment, so In/Out must never alter its geometry).
- **Quadcopter physics model with make/model catalogs** (`39f10b17`, `8bc109f8`, `bcb179df`) (`src/QuadcopterModel.js`, `src/VehicleModels.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeLOSFitPhysics.js`, `src/JetStuff.js`). The **Physics Model** selector (Traverse menu) gains **Quadcopter** — a new hover-capable multirotor model (`QuadcopterModel.js`) alongside Sky Lantern and Fixed Wing Aircraft. A new `VehicleModels.js` catalog backs two new sub-dropdowns — **Quadcopter Model** (DJI Mini 4 Pro, Air 3, Mavic 3, Phantom 4 Pro, DJI FPV, Racing FPV) and **Fixed-Wing Model** (Cessna 172, MQ-9 Reaper, Boeing 737-800, F/A-18E/F, F-35, F-16) — of which only the one matching the selected physics model is shown. Picking a specific entry tightens the fit bounds to that airframe's real envelope (readout "Selected envelope prior"); leaving it on Auto fits the generic envelope and `classifyQuadcopter`/`classifyFixedWing` name the nearest real type from the solved speed/climb (readout "Closest compatible envelope"). Classification is direction-aware from the start: a solved descent is checked against `maxDescent` (usually the smaller capability) with signed climb, plus a ceiling check; load factor is approximated from maneuver acceleration before structural-g comparisons. The quadcopter fit reports Start Range, Heading (origin ENU), Start Speed, Peak Speed, and Climb.
- **Stationary-point and ground-based traverse methods** (`8bc109f8`, `86ba9f9c`, `bcb179df`) (`src/nodes/CNodeLOSFitStationaryPoint.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeLOSFitGroundVehicle.js`, `src/TraverseAnalysis.js`, `src/MakeTraverseNodesMenu.js`). Three new **LOS Traverse Method** options, the live counterparts of the gallery's tiles so "Use This" reproduces a tile exactly: **Global Fit: Stationary Point** (`CNodeLOSFitStationaryPoint` — the closed-form fixed world point that best fits every sightline; no on-ray traverse can hold a fixed point, walking rays at speed 0 still moves by the rays' closest-approach distance each frame), **Global Fit: Ground Object** (the same node with `groundPin` — pinned to a curved constant-elevation shell at terrain height, not the abandoned sea-level z=0 model), and **Ground Vehicle** (`CNodeLOSFitGroundVehicle` — each sightline intersected with the curved constant-elevation shell, giving a point moving along the ground). The analysis-side math (`TraverseAnalysis.js`) performs the matching curved-Earth constant-altitude/ground-vehicle/ground-point fits with geodetic altitude and vertical-speed metrics, pinned by `tests/StationaryGroundFits.test.js`.
- **Ground-contact analysis modes** (`dc02e7c6`, `bcb179df`) (`src/AnalyzeTraverse.js`, `src/LOSFitting.js`). New **Ground contact** dropdown in Traverse → Traverse Analysis Tweaks: *Airborne (any)*, *Starts on ground*, *Ends on ground*, *On the ground*. The non-airborne modes feed a soft ground prior into `fitPhysicsModel` (`options.groundPrior`, σ = 40 m, curvature-corrected tangent-plane height) pulling the trajectory's start and/or end altitude toward the local ground; "On the ground" adds a Ground Vehicle candidate and demotes airborne solutions (`groundContactViolation`); underground trajectories are always rejected. Gated on the option — default fits are byte-identical.

### Improvements
- **Deterministic, cancellable, self-diagnosing traverse fits** (`114b2a45`, `dc02e7c6`, `86ba9f9c`) (`src/DifferentialEvolution.js`, `src/NelderMead.js`, `src/BoundedFit.js`, `src/LOSFitting.js`, `src/WindFromConstantAirspeed.js`). Differential evolution accepts an injected PRNG (`options.rng`; `mulberry32` exported); `fitPhysicsModel` seeds it (`options.seed`, default `0xF17DE5`) and records optimizer metadata (seeds, evaluation counts, stop reasons, bound pins) — previously unseeded `Math.random` meant user-visible fitted parameters could flip between runs on near-degenerate scenes. Monte Carlo 1/2 and the wind-from-camera GA (seed `0x57494E44`) are seeded the same way, so identical inputs now give identical results per click. `options.shouldCancel` is wired to the analysis overlay's Cancel button, which now actually stops in-flight physics fits. Nelder-Mead steps its initial simplex INWARD at parameter bounds (a pinned start no longer collapses the simplex volume) and requires parameter-space convergence in addition to cost spread. New `BoundedFit.assessBoundPins` probes each near-bound parameter inward and only demotes a result when the bound is locally load-bearing — inactive pins are reported, not penalized (the gallery's "Model limit hit" vs "Unconstrained at bound" badges). `tests/LOSFittingDeterminism.test.js` pins bit-identical repeat runs through the production path.
- **Sim Speed is physical time everywhere** (`67473cf5`, `2a62da42`, `8bc109f8`, `bcb179df`) (`src/nodes/CNodeJetTrack.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeWind.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeLOSTraverseConstantAltitude.js`, `src/TraverseAnalysisData.js`, `src/JetGraphs.js`, `src/CCustomGraphManager.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeSimInfoUI.js`). Per-frame motion now consistently uses physical dt = `simSpeed`/fps: the simulated jet track (airspeed step, turn integration, forced headings, racetrack phase), per-frame wind displacement, sequential traverses (Constant Altitude accumulates vertical speed from frame 0 at physical dt and intersects the WGS84 ellipsoid at geodetic altitude), the analysis window's effective fps (`Sit.fps / simSpeed`), the speed/g graphs, and the Sim Info traverse readouts. At the default Sim Speed of 1 all geometry and readouts are unchanged; at other values, speeds, g-forces and track geometry now stay physically consistent with the accelerated/decelerated clock instead of silently reporting frame-rate quantities.
- **war.gov UAP catalog updated to Release 4** (`34edbe2c`) (`data/WARGOV/uap-data.csv`). The bundled snapshot topped out at PR-099, so newer PR-coded cases (e.g. PR101, South China Sea 2024) failed to resolve when dropping war.gov, DVIDS, or Metabunk-thread URLs, falling through to the "Unsupported URL" prompt (`DragDropHandler.js`). The Release 4 snapshot adds PR100–PR116 plus the previously missing PR024/PR030. The feed's new leading "Featured" column is harmless — `parseWarGovCSV` (`WarGovUFOUtils.js`) keys records by header name.

### Bug Fixes
- **Fixed the wind fitted from a camera track being mirrored east-west** (`86ba9f9c`) (`src/WindFromConstantAirspeed.js`). `sampleGroundVelocities` built its local east basis as `up × north` — which is WEST — so every wind bearing fitted by the **Local from Constant Camera** button (Wind menu) was mirrored about the north-south axis (reported = 360° − true; e.g. a true wind FROM 296.6° was reported as from 63.4°). Fixed with `getLocalEastVector`, and pinned by a ground-truth bearing test in `tests/WindFromConstantAirspeed.test.js`. The refinement GA is also seeded now (see the determinism entry), and its sample rate honors the track's own fps and `simSpeed`.
- **Fixed track-driven wind sampling the playhead instead of the frame** (`67473cf5`) (`src/nodes/CNodeWind.js`, `src/nodes/WindHelpers.js`). For winds bound to a loaded track, `CNodeWind.getValueFrame` ignored its frame argument and returned whatever `from`/`knots` the playhead's `update()` had last written — so traverse analyses, wind-drifted traverses and air-speed graphs silently used ONE wind value across the entire clip, and the value depended on where playback sat. New pure per-frame samplers `trackWindAt(f)` / `windVectorAt(f, position)` return the wind the track carried AT each frame, on a validated time axis (the shared PES clock when available, a monotonicity-checked wall clock otherwise, or a frame-mapped fallback), interpolating the meteorological vector between rows (through north, so 359°→1° takes the short path); frames in a wind-data gap use the nearest row with data. `WindHelpers.normalizeWindTimestampMs` normalizes mixed-unit (µs/ms/s) timestamps. Consumers: `buildAnalysisDataset`, `CNodeTrackAir`, `CNodeTraverseAngularSpeed`, and `getValueFrame` itself; `update()` now cascades only when the underlying track ROW changes. Pinned by the new `tests/nodes/CNodeWind.test.js` suite.
- **Fixed video-tracking sightlines depending on window geometry** (`312560a1`) (`src/nodes/CNodeTrackingOverlay.js`). The manual-tracking overlay derived its per-frame LOS by round-tripping through the live canvas — letterbox `fovCoverage`, `widthPx`/`heightPx` from `div.clientWidth`, zoom, and a panOffset zero/restore hack — purely to invert the display mapping, and mixed `zoom.v0` with `zoom.v(f)`. The transform cancels analytically (verified for both letterbox branches), so the LOS is now computed entirely in original-video pixel space: `fpx = originalVideoHeight / (2·tan(vFOV/2))`, angles from the pixel offsets to the frame center. Tracking-based analyses are now reproducible across window sizes, layouts and zoom/pan states.
- **Fixed the analysis's parabolic range refinement never refining** (`86ba9f9c`) (`src/TraverseAnalysis.js`). The two-pass parabolic refine in log-range around the coarse minimum mixed centerings in its vertex formula and returned the unrefined grid point; replaced with the exact, unit-tested `parabolicVertex` helper, so reported best-fit start ranges are refined between grid samples as intended.

### Documentation
- **Traverse methods spec rewritten to match shipped behavior** (`215b3d4a`) (`docs/TraverseMethods.md`, Help → Documentation → Traverse Methods via `docsRegistry.js`). Documents the A-B window semantics, physical time (short-window differentiation clamp, NaN-not-zero invalid metrics), frame-pure historical wind with nearest-row gap fallback, direction-aware quadcopter envelope checks, and the analysis-integrity contract (grouped ranking, family bands, bound sensitivity, circular-LOS labelling). Also adds `docs/TraverseAnalysisReview.md`, the internal review ledger for this release's traverse work (14 confirmed findings and 5 minor notes, all fixed pre-release).

## Version 2.100.0 (2026-07-09)

### New Features
- **Orbit camera gains a dedicated Orbit Altitude control** (working tree) (`src/CustomManagerSetup.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeOrbitTrack.js`). New `orbitAltitude` `CNodeGUIValue` (label **Orbit Altitude (m)**, range 1–20000, step 1, default 1000) in Camera → Camera Tweaks, placed after Orbit Radius. `CNodeOrbitTrack` now wires its `altitude` input to this slider and reads it as a plain scalar HAE via `this.in.altitude.getValueFrame(0)`, replacing the old behaviour of taking altitude per-frame from the manual camera position node (`fixedCameraPosition`) via `ECEFToLLAVD_radii(this.in.altitude.p(f)).z`. The manual camera's "Cam [C] Alt" control is disabled/greyed while the camera follows a track, so the orbit altitude was effectively un-editable; the dedicated slider is always live. Backward-compat: `setupMethods` seeds the slider from the fixed camera's current HAE (`ECEFToLLAVD_radii(fixedCameraPosition.p(0)).z`) when the load is a saved sitch (`Sit.mods`) that has no stored `orbitAltitude`, running before `CustomManager.deserialize()` so a genuinely saved value still wins — preserving each old sitch's original orbit framing. The same commit also widens Orbit Radius from `start 100 / step 100` to `start 1 / step 1`.
- **Orbit camera gains a Start Angle control** (working tree) (`src/CustomManagerSetup.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeOrbitTrack.js`). New `orbitStartAngle` `CNodeGUIValue` (label **Start Angle (°)**, 0–360, step 0.1, default 0) in Camera Tweaks, wired to `CNodeOrbitTrack`'s new optional `startAngle` input. The orbit angle — already measured clockwise from north (`cos`→north, `sin`→east) — gains this value as a phase offset (`startAngleRad + 2π·f/(period·fps)`), so 0° starts due north (the historical behaviour), 90° east, 180° south, 270° west. Default 0 reproduces the old north-start exactly.
- **Orthographic camera mode for the main and look views** (working tree) (`src/nodes/CNodeCamera.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeBuildings3DTiles.js`). New per-camera **Main View Orthographic** / **Look View Orthographic** checkboxes plus a **Main Near Plane (m)** slider (0.1–2000, main camera only — the look camera's near plane is owned by its PTZ controller) in Camera → Camera Tweaks (`addCameraTweaksControls`). Implemented WITHOUT swapping the camera object: `_installOrthographicOverride` replaces `PerspectiveCamera.updateProjectionMatrix` with an instance override that builds an orthographic matrix while `orthographic` is on and calls the original perspective update otherwise, so every `.fov` / `.aspect` / `isPerspectiveCamera` reader in the app keeps working. `_updateOrthographicProjectionMatrix` sizes the ortho box so its half-height equals what the perspective frustum spans at a reference distance (`tan(fov/2)·d/zoom`), making a toggle visually seamless and scaling the ortho zoom as the camera dollies. The reference distance `_orthoRefDistance` is the camera→ground distance under the view centre, measured each frame by `_refreshOrthoProjection` via `raycastLocalGround(raycaster, camera)` against the real terrain mesh AND the Google 3D-tile surface — only a real-surface hit (`isTerrain`) is used for sizing, never the ellipsoid fallback, so buildings near the screen centre stay at ~perspective scale. Because three.js's log-depth shader falls back to `gl_FragCoord.z` for a non-perspective matrix, the ortho far plane is a tight distance-scaled `near + 8·d` (not the camera's ~1e9 m perspective far) to keep linear depth precise and avoid z-fighting on rooftops; `near` stays the user's Near Plane so it still slices. `orthographic` and `nearPlane` are serialized (`modSerialize`/`modDeserialize`) and reasserted per-frame in `update()`. In `CNodeBuildings3DTiles`, the per-view tile "settle" fingerprint (`PerViewTiles`) now folds in projection-matrix terms `p[0]+p[5]+p[10]+p[14]` alongside world pose + fov/zoom/aspect, so an ortho toggle / near-plane change / ortho-scale change with a static camera re-traverses the tile LOD instead of leaving stale tiles until the camera next moves.

### Improvements
- **Orbit / pan pivot lands on Google 3D buildings** (working tree) (`src/raycastGround.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeView3DMouse.js`, `src/js/CameraControls.js`). `raycastLocalGround(raycaster, camera?)` gained an optional `camera` argument. When supplied and `buildings3DTiles` has loaded geometry, it also intersects the shared tiles group — temporarily widening the raycaster's layer mask to the camera's (the tile meshes live on the MAIN/LOOK layers, not layer 0) and setting `firstHitOnly` — and keeps the NEAREST of the terrain-mesh hit and the tile hit. Previously only the terrain mesh plus an ellipsoid fallback were considered, so orbiting/panning near tall geometry pivoted around the terrain hidden behind the building. The two pivot call sites now pass the camera: `CNodeView3DMouse._refreshCursorFromMouseInner` (mouse-down cursor/pivot refresh) and `CameraControls.updateTargetFromScreenPosition` (touch pivot). Result: shift-drag orbit and middle-mouse pan rotate around the rooftop/wall directly under the cursor. `isTerrain` stays true for any concrete surface (terrain OR building) and false only for the ellipsoid fallback, so CameraControls' flat-drag-plane-vs-globe-sphere choice treats a rooftop like terrain.

### Bug Fixes
- **Fixed double-clicking a Time-panel date/time slider resetting it to NaN** (working tree) (`src/nodes/CNodeDateTime.js`, `src/EXIFUtils.js`, `src/CustomManagerSetup.js`, `src/CSitrecAPI.js`). Double-clicking a slider label resets it to that controller's `_defaultValue` (see `lil-gui-slider-settings.js`), but the seven Time sliders (Year/Month/Day/Hour/Minute/Second/Millisecond) are built before `this.dateTime` is populated, so `_defaultValue` was captured as `undefined` and a double-click wrote NaN into the field. Each slider now gets a canonical baseline (`_defaultValue`: current UTC year, month 1, day 1, time components 0). A new `CNodeDateTime.establishDateTimeDefaults()` re-captures the currently displayed date/time as the reset target for all seven sliders whenever the date is AUTHORITATIVELY established, and is called from `populateStartTimeFromUTCString` (loading a sitch — fresh, serialized, or URL override), `modSerialize` (saving a sitch), `applyImportedImageCaptureDateTime` in `EXIFUtils.js` (dropped image's embedded EXIF date), the dropped-video `meta.creationDate` path in `CustomManagerSetup.js`, and `CSitrecAPI.js` (chatbot setting the date). It is deliberately NOT called on plain slider edits or live-mode ticks, so a double-click means "revert to the last established date" rather than "revert to whatever was last dragged."

## Version 2.99.0 (2026-07-08)

### New Features
- **Sim Info overlay gains six Traverse readouts** (working tree) (`src/nodes/CNodeSimInfoUI.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeVideoInfoUI.js`, `src/i18n/en.js`). The Sim Info Display overlay (`CNodeSimInfoUI`, Show → Sim Info Display, drawn on the look view) adds six independently-toggleable, draggable text readouts derived per-frame from the selected traverse track — `LOSTraverseSelect`, falling back to `LOSTraverseSelectTrack` for custom sitches (`getTraverseNode()`) — the same data source the Graphs *Traverse Speed* / *Traverse Altitude* / *Object g-force* plots use:
  - **Traverse Speed** — total 3D object speed.
  - **Traverse Ground Speed** — horizontal ground speed (the vertical/ellipsoid-up component is projected out).
  - **Traverse Air Speed** — horizontal air speed (the per-frame `targetWind` displacement is subtracted, then the result is projected onto the ground plane; the line is hidden entirely when there is no `targetWind` track, so air speed is never shown without wind data).
  - **Traverse g-force** — total g (second difference of position × fps² ÷ 9.81, matching `CNodeGForce`, clamped near the clip end).
  - **Traverse Alt (MSL)** — altitude above mean sea level = HAE − geoid offset (`meanSeaLevelOffset`).
  - **Traverse Alt (AGL)** — object HAE − ground-surface HAE directly below, preferring the loaded 3D-tile surface (`getTilesPointBelow`, so it rides buildings/photogrammetry the elevation map ignores), falling back to the elevation map, then the geoid; using HAE for both points makes the difference datum-independent.
  All six default **OFF**; speeds honor the Units setting via `Units.m2Speed` / `Units.speed.abbrev` (kt / mph / km/h) and altitudes use `Units.smallWithUnits` (ft / m). The three speed lines share one `traverseSpeedText(frame, mode)` helper. Implemented via a new base-class hook `CNodeVideoInfoUI.drawExtraItems(drawTextWithBg, frame)` (no-op in the base, called from the base draw path) so the subclass reuses the base overlay's font / background-box / positioning layout, its drag hit-testing (`getElementBounds` / `getElementPos` / `getShowProp`) and auto-stacking (`positionItemToAvoidOverlaps`). The six show flags and their X/Y positions are serialized (`addSimpleSerial`); labels and tooltips are added under `simInfo.*` in `src/i18n/en.js`.
- **"G" = Go to Frame** (`3b5eb2d5`) (`src/updateFrame.js`, `src/showError.js`). Pressing **G** outside a text field opens a styled prompt for a frame number, jumps there and pauses (`par.paused = true`, `GlobalDateTimeNode.liveMode = false`), clamping to the sitch length (`lastSitFrame()`) and expanding the In/Out range (`Sit.aFrame` / `Sit.bFrame`) if the target falls outside it. Backed by a new `showPrompt(message, opts)` in `showError.js` — a Promise-based, styled replacement for the native blocking `prompt()` (which freezes the main thread and stalls automation/MCP); it resolves to the entered string or `null` (Cancel/Escape/backdrop), and resolves to `null` immediately in `Globals.validationMode` so regression runs don't hang.

### Improvements
- **Sitch Frames slider live-follows the Out marker** (`3b5eb2d5`) (`src/nodes/CNodeDateTime.js`). While dragging the Sitch Frames slider, if the Out frame was sitting at the last frame it is now carried to the new last frame on every `onChange` tick (`Sit.bFrame = newFrames - 1` when `Sit.bFrame === this.lastFrames - 1`), with the In/Out sliders' maxima and the frame slider updated live — so lengthening a sitch keeps the Out marker glued to the end instead of leaving it behind (matching the existing `changedFrames`-on-release behavior).
- **Altitude readouts show MSL, HAE and the geoid offset** (`3b5eb2d5`) (`src/CustomManagerMenus.js`, `src/CFeatureManager.js`, `src/i18n/en.js`). The ground right-click context menu now prints coordinates, then MSL altitude, then WGS84 HAE with the HAE−MSL difference (the EGM96 geoid offset N, `meanSeaLevelOffset`, negative across most of CONUS) in parentheses. The feature-edit dialog replaces its single mislabeled "Altitude" row (which actually displayed HAE) with two read-only rows — **Altitude MSL (m)** and **Altitude HAE (m)** (the HAE value followed by N in parentheses) — so the dialog and the ground menu agree and name their datum explicitly. New i18n keys `featureManager.altitudeMSL` / `featureManager.altitudeHAE`.
- **Default overlay font size raised 30 → 40** (working tree) (`src/nodes/CNodeVideoInfoUI.js`). The shared `fontSize` default in `CNodeVideoInfoUI` (inherited by `CNodeSimInfoUI`) is now `v.fontSize ?? 40`, enlarging the default text of both the Video Info Display and the Sim Info Display overlays. Default-only: any overlay with a previously saved `fontSize` keeps its existing size.
- **Removed the redundant "L" key and refreshed the on-screen shortcut list** (`3b5eb2d5`) (`src/mouseMoveView.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeView3DMouse.js`, `src/nodes/CNodeControllerVarious.js`, `src/nodes/CNodePositionLLA.js`, `src/JetStuff.js`). "L" duplicated "C" (camera position-LLA cursor snap), so it is dropped from the cursor-refresh key gate and from the manual-position / position-LLA / mouse-view controllers (stale "lock-all" comments updated). The keyboard-shortcut HUD (`UpdateHUD`) now lists **G** (Go to Frame), **I** (Set In Frame), **O** (Set Out Frame) and **K** (Show/Hide Shortcuts).

### Bug Fixes
- **Corrected units on MISB tag 104 (SensorEllipsoidHeightExtended)** (`d63284e4`) (`src/MISBUtils.js`). The `misbTagInfo` entry for ST0601 tag 104 declared `units: "Pixels"`; it is now `"Meters"`. This field is not currently read anywhere in Sitrec, so there is no user-visible effect — the correction keeps the tag table right for any future use of tag 104.

## Version 2.98.3 (2026-07-08)

### Bug Fixes
- **Altitude-datum (MSL vs HAE) column selection is now reset per call and decided across all rows** (`3416d5f0`) (`src/nodes/CNodeMISBData.js`). `CNodeMISBDataTrack.selectSourceColumns()` chooses which MISB column supplies altitude and sets `altitudeIsHAE` (true = the value is already height-above-ellipsoid, so the geoid-offset add in `needsGeoidToHAE()` is skipped). Two correctness bugs from the 2.98.1 datum work are fixed. (1) The source-declared datum (e.g. STANAG `cs="WGS_84"`) is now stored separately as `_baseAltitudeIsHAE`, and `altitudeIsHAE` is reset to that base at the top of every `selectSourceColumns()` call. Previously, once a call fell back to an ellipsoid-height column (ST0601 tag 75/78) and promoted `altitudeIsHAE` to `true`, the promotion stuck, so a later call that reselected a populated MSL column kept the stale HAE flag, skipped the geoid add, and placed the track off by the geoid height N. (2) The "is the MSL column empty?" test previously only inspected row 0 (`this.misb[0][this.altCol] === null`), so a null first row wrongly triggered the AGL/HAE fallback even when the rest of the column had data. A new `_columnHasData(col)` helper scans every row for any non-null/undefined value; both the MSL-empty test and the ellipsoid-height availability test now use it instead of trusting row 0.
- **Derived frame-center track keeps Frame Center Height Above Ellipsoid (tag 78) when Frame Center Elevation (tag 25) is absent** (`3416d5f0`) (`src/TrackFiles/CTrackFileMISB.js`, `tests/CTrackFileMISB.test.js`). `CTrackFileMISB.toMISB(1)` builds the derived frame-center (ground aim-point) track from the `FrameCenter*` columns. It used to write `SensorTrueAltitude = FrameCenterElevation ?? 0`, so a source that carried only `FrameCenterHeightAboveEllipsoid` (ST0601 tag 78, HAE) and no `FrameCenterElevation` (tag 25, MSL) lost its altitude entirely and the frame-center track dropped to 0. It now prefers tag 25 into `SensorTrueAltitude`; when tag 25 is absent but tag 78 is present it writes the HAE value into `SensorEllipsoidHeight`, so the data track's datum-aware column selection flags it HAE and does not add the geoid offset to an already-ellipsoidal value; only when neither tag is present does it fall back to 0. A new unit test confirms a tag-78-only frame center yields `SensorEllipsoidHeight` preserved (480) and `SensorTrueAltitude` null rather than a bogus 0.

---

## Version 2.98.2 (2026-07-07)

### Improvements
- **Contents-menu track folders show the clean display name, not the node id** (`7f0fac00`) (`src/TrackManager.js`, `src/lil-gui-extras.js`). Each loaded track's folder in the Contents menu is now created with `guiMenus.contents.addFolder(shortName)` (e.g. `elevated_track (Platform)`) instead of `addFolder(trackID)` (`Track_elevated_track (Platform)`). The folder stashes its node id in a new `guiFolder._lookupId = trackID`, and `GUI.prototype.getFolder(title)` now matches on `child._lookupId === title || child.$title.innerText === title`, so `CNodeDisplayTrack`'s lookup-by-node-id (`menu.getFolder(track.id)`) still resolves the same folder and no duplicate empty folders are created. This is a general menu change affecting **all** file-loaded tracks, not just STANAG.
- **STANAG sub-track roles revised: the tracked object is the Target, the ground point is an unroled reference** (`7f0fac00`) (`src/TrackFiles/CTrackFileSTANAG.js`). In `_distinctTracks()`'s three-position (`_hasPosLowHigh`) branch, `dynamics/pos` now gets suffix ` (Target)` and role `"target"` (was suffix `""`, role `null`), `posHigh` stays ` (Platform)` / role `"camera"`, and `posLow` stays ` (Ground)` but its role becomes `null` (was `"target"`). `trackRoleHint()` follows the new map. This revises 2.98.1's `posLow → target` assignment: the target track is now the standard's authoritative tracked-object estimate rather than the LOS ground-intersection aim point. Because `dynamics/pos`, `posLow` and `posHigh` are collinear, the camera at Platform points identically whether it aims at Target or Ground, so this is a labelling/role-assignment change with no geometric effect. A bare dynamics-only STANAG file (no `posLow`/`posHigh`) is unchanged — it stays the roleless plain primary track.
- **STANAG import offers "target track only" when a camera line-of-sight is already loaded** (`65042ab3`) (`src/TrackManager.js`). When a track file that carries a target-role track plus extra reference tracks (a STANAG 4676 file's target + platform + ground) is dragged in while a camera line-of-sight already exists, an import-time choice dialog now appears instead of silently loading every sub-track. An existing camera LOS is detected by iterating `TrackManager` for any track whose `anglesController` or `anglesNode` is set (a MISB/KLV track with sensor angles builds these). The "target + extras" file is detected generically via a local `targetTrackIndex(file)` helper that scans `CTrackFile.trackRoleHint(i)` for a `"target"` role; the new branch is an `else if` after the existing `getImportTrackCount() >= 3` multi-track picker and is gated on `cameraLOSLoaded && tIdx >= 0 && trackCount >= 2`, so it only fires for STANAG's grouped 2-3 sub-tracks (which deliberately stay under the picker threshold). The dialog (`showChoice`, title *Load STANAG Track*) offers *Target track only* — which sets `selectedIndicesMap` for the file to `new Set([tIdx])` so the main load loop skips the platform/ground sub-tracks — and *Load all tracks*. Dismiss/Escape and the validation harness resolve to `cancelValue: "all"` (leave `selectedIndicesMap` unset → load every sub-track), so existing import flows and regression baselines are unchanged. The platform and ground reference tracks are redundant with the already-loaded camera's line of sight; choosing target-only makes the tracked target available as a Target-track option for comparison without auto-selecting it, so the established sitch's existing camera aim is preserved.

### Bug Fixes
- **Fixed importing a STANAG file wrongly showing the multi-track *Select Tracks* dialog** (`604d82a3`) (`src/TrackFiles/CTrackFile.js`, `src/TrackFiles/CTrackFileSTANAG.js`, `src/TrackManager.js`). A STANAG 4676 file's Platform/dynamics/Ground sub-tracks all derive from one NATO track and always load together, but `getTrackCount()` returns 3 for an elevated target, so `TrackManager`'s `getTrackCount() >= 3` gate triggered `showMultiTrackLoadDialog` (the *Select Tracks* picker). New `CTrackFile.getImportTrackCount()` (base returns `getTrackCount()`) expresses the count of **independently-selectable** tracks; `CTrackFileSTANAG` overrides it to the NATO track count (`data.nitsRoot.message.numTracks`, default 1). The dialog gate now tests `file.getImportTrackCount() >= 3`, so a lone STANAG track loads all its sub-tracks directly with no picker. KML/MISB files are unchanged because their two counts are equal. Reading `numTracks` means the picker will fire automatically if multi-`<track>` STANAG support is ever added.
- **Fixed the look camera not aiming at the target after a STANAG import (heading not set to *To Target*)** (`7f0fac00`) (`src/TrackManager.js`). Now that the STANAG target is the primary (`trackNumber === 1`) track, the existing "first track → Manual heading" default was clobbering the *To Target* heading that the target auto-assignment (`updateDropTargets`) had just set. The default is now skipped when the first track was itself auto-assigned as the target: `const firstTrackIsTarget = targetSwitch && targetSwitch.choice === shortName` guards the `!hasAngles && trackNumber === 1` branch (`targetTrackSwitch` / `CameraLOSController`), so the STANAG camera keeps its *To Target* heading.
